But ever since then, I just get that weird feeling when I stand near the glass or look out at that part of the street. I hope I'm not overreacting by being bothered by it.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself reliving the car crash in your mind, like it's happening again?\n\nPatient: Um... well, I really don't want to make it sound like I'm losing my mind or anything, because I know it's over and done with. I'll just be standing there, and suddenly it's like I can hear that awful bang again, and... and I can almost see the glass coming down, just like it's happening right then. \n\nI try to just shake it off right away and get back to work. I know it's just my imagination playing tricks on me because I was so startled when it happened. It's not like I'm hallucinating or anything crazy like that, I promise. It's just a quick flash, really. Is it okay that I'm still having those little flashes sometimes? I'm really trying my best to just ignore them.\n\nDoctor: Do you avoid talking or thinking about the car crash that happened at the store?\n\nPatient: Um... well, no, not really. Um... well, I wouldn't say I avoid *everything* like that. I don't want to sound like I'm hiding from the world, you know? I still watch the news and everything.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... no, not really. I mean, I try not to dwell on it, but I wouldn't say I'm actively avoiding thinking about it. 

I just like to keep busy, you know? When I'm at home, I try to focus on positive things, like watching a movie or talking to my friends. I don't think it's healthy to sit around and obsess over bad things that have happened, right? So I just try to keep my mind occupied. It's better to focus on moving forward, isn't it? I just want to make sure I'm handling everything the right way.

Um... no, not intentionally. I mean, if people ask, I'll answer them. I don't want to seem rude or closed off.

Um... well, maybe a little bit? But I think that's just because retail can be so chaotic, you know? It's totally normal to be a little jumpy when you work in a busy store.
